# Salt-anchored parameter handoff

Issue #435. This inventory records numerical evidence and open manuscript replacements. Parameter promotion requires delivered-evidence review.

TeX source revision `75c3ee525d9068a37197bbb1e915990f7a87b59a`; Round 5 source revision `804811e44eadfb04f1e3a51a6be0a46a4c54ba00`; post-#434 implementation base `50cbeaf006aa378ae212986c29caf1de9c1d42a5`.

- reference source law: 40 source occurrences.
- unresolved: 3989 source occurrences.

The ledger covers source tokens. Rendered numbering, macro expansion and display readback still require reconciliation.
